Task ID: 58-a (engine v2)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: v1.18.0 — Rust engine "full potential" speed work + fix CLI fallback + icon fixes + installer-only build.

Work Log:
- Research (41 web searches, primary sources): NVIDIA SDK-10 preset ladders, QSV/AMF settings, wgpu readback ring-buffering + storage-buffer YUV packing, FFmpeg thread-safety rules, pipeline architecture, electron icon bugs (AUMID, electron-builder #2435 long-description NSIS blank shortcut).
- CRITICAL BUGS FOUND in v1 engine: (1) h264_qsv tier opened with YUV420P but QSV ONLY accepts NV12 → Intel machines silently fell back to single-threaded libx264; (2) cc_set_threads_auto never called on the PRIMARY encoder → libx264 ran SINGLE-THREADED; (3) NVENC delay=0 forced synchronous packet-per-frame (killed NVENC pipelining); (4) per-layer queue.submit + full-cache wipe at 32 textures + per-frame texture alloc for video; (5) audio decode/mix serialized AFTER the video loop.
- rust-engine v2 (engine version 0.2.0): 3-stage pipeline (decode-ahead producer thread → bounded sync_channel(4) → composite+encode consumer; audio thread decodes/mixes IN PARALLEL, joined at the audio phase); ONE encoder+submit per frame with dynamic-offset uniform batching; LRU static texture cache + pooled dynamic video slots; compute-shader RGBA→YUV420P/NV12 (BT.601 limited = sws parity, packed 1.5B/px readback, no CPU sws on GPU path); AVFrame ring; per-encoder pix_fmt (QSV/AMF/MF=NV12); x264 threads auto; NVENC p3/p4/p6+multipass/lookahead ladders, no delay=0; EAGAIN send/drain retry contract; movflags +faststart; WARP/software adapter rejection (CI override FRAMEFUSE_ENGINE_ALLOW_SOFTWARE_GPU=1).
- Timeline schema v2: extraAudio (VO+SFX native bus) + per-segment transition plan (transHeadMs/Style, transTailMs/Style, bookendStart/End).
- NATIVE transitions: dissolve (prev image at frozen Ken Burns end-state + current ramping, VIDEO RULE hard-cut, overlays not cross-faded — CLI layering parity) + dip-black/dip-white head/tail + fadeStartEnd bookends; captions stay above fades (the layering contract).
- Router: voiceovers+SFX gates REMOVED (extraAudio); dubDuck applied router-side (mirror of main.js math); transition gate split — dissolve/dips native, slide/wipe/circleopen → CLI; per-seg transition dead gate removed; progress events carry engine:"rust".
- main.js: app.setAppUserModelId("com.framefuse.app") (the taskbar blank-white fix — matches electron-builder's shortcut AUMI); CLI progress carries engine:"cli"; export-info gains rustEngine status.
- Renderer: Header engine badge (Rust engine | FFmpeg CLI) during export; types extended; version strings → 1.18.0.
- ICONS: package.json description 430→62 chars (electron-builder #2435: long descriptions overflow the NSIS CreateShortCut command → blank shortcut icon); installer.nsh customInstall recreates desktop+start-menu shortcuts with EXPLICIT $INSTDIR\resources\icon.ico icons + WinShell::SetLnkAUMI + SHChangeNotify + ie4uinit (customInstall runs AFTER addDesktopLink in the electron-builder template — verified).

Task ID: 58-b (the black-frame bug)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: GPU-path verification of engine v2 → found + fixed THE v1.16-era black-frame bug.

Work Log:
- Installed rustup + mesa-vulkan-drivers (extracted to /tmp, custom VK_ICD_FILENAMES with llvmpipe) on the sandbox → FIRST-EVER local GPU-path test capability. With FRAMEFUSE_ENGINE_ALLOW_SOFTWARE_GPU=1 the engine ran "rust-gpu" on llvmpipe.
- CI (WARP) first caught a wgpu Validation Error (dynamic-offset binding used as_entire_binding → "maximum the binding can be offset is 0 bytes") + the uniform buffer was sized 96×MAX_LAYERS instead of slot-stride×MAX_LAYERS. Fixed (explicit BufferBinding size=96; correct buffer size).
- GPU path then rendered BLACK (clear worked, layers invisible). Isolation ladder: constant-color fragment → no draw; hardcoded fullscreen vertex → DRAWS (255,51,26); uniform-buffer dump → data present + correct; non-dynamic binding → still black; git-stash v1 engine → ALSO BLACK. → PRE-EXISTING since v1.16.0.
- ROOT CAUSE: WGSL uniform layout gives mat3x3<f32> 16-byte COLUMN STRIDES (vec3 columns padded to vec4 — col0@0..12, col1@16..28, col2@32..44). v1 wrote the 9 floats PACKED (36 contiguous bytes) → shader read col1/col2 from wrong offsets → transform degenerated to y'=0, w=0 (zero-area triangles) → NOTHING rasterized → BLACK FRAMES from the GPU compositor since v1.16.0. CI never caught it (frame-count assertions pass on black video). This explains years of "rust-gpu" CI greens and very plausibly the user's "export falls back / engine not working properly" reports.
- FIX: write_layer_uniform writes strided columns (0/16/32). All GPU tests green on llvmpipe: color test exact (red 81/90/240, blue 41/240/110, white 235/128, green 81/90/80 — ffmpeg color names are the half-brightness CSS set, expectation table fixed), smoke YAVG 124.0→80.5 (CPU: 124.0→79.9 — GPU/CPU parity), v2 feature test (dissolve f92=73 ramp, dip f178=16, bookend, VO -43.1dB) PASSED on rust-gpu.
- New permanent regression tests: rust-engine/tools/yuv-color-test.js (solid-color center pixel through the FULL engine, both paths) + the smoke test's signalstats YAVG assertions — black-frame regressions can never pass silently again. Color test added to CI (with the WARP override).
- Committed a3c202b + pushed; CI run 36908650583 in progress.

Stage Summary:
- The GPU compositor is now VERIFIED CORRECT for the first time in the project's history (v1.16-v1.17 shipped a silently-black GPU path; the three-level fallback chain masked it).
- Local GPU test rig: rustup + llvmpipe ICD at /tmp/lvp-icd/lvp_icd.json (VK_ICD_FILENAMES) + FRAMEFUSE_ENGINE_ALLOW_SOFTWARE_GPU=1 — future rounds can test rust-gpu on the sandbox.
- The dynamic-offset single-pass batching is RESTORED and verified working (with strided matrices).

ONE Style KineticC<idx> (idx = composition array index): Fontname = resolve(settings.fontOverride || preset.fontId), Fontsize = geo.fontPx, Primary = hexToAssColor(textColor), Back = &H73000000 (canvas shadow rgba(0,0,0,.55)), Bold −1 iff emphasisWeight ≥ 600, Outline = max(1, round(2·H/1080)) (thin §26 stroke — canvas presets are shadow-only, ASS keeps the modern readable stroke), Shadow = max(1, round(3·H/1080)) iff preset.shadow, Alignment 5, Margins 0, Spacing = round(trackingFrac·fontPx).

PER-WORD (slide-x, burst, converge, typewriter) → ONE Dialogue PER WORD: \an5\move(from → measured word CENTER, 0, entranceMs) — from-vectors mirror motion.ts exactly: slide-x ±56·energy (dir from phrase align, else wordIdx alternating), burst burstVector(idx)·64/46·energy·roleEnergy, converge ·90/60·energy; typewriter = static \pos + the Dialogue start time IS the reveal; burst/converge add \fscx86/90→100 settle.
  - \t/\move times are MILLISECONDS RELATIVE TO THE DIALOGUE START (libass semantics — critical): inline line Dialogue starts at its first word's entrance d (word times relative to winStart, d = max(compStart, wordStart−60) + phraseIdx·stagger [+ idx·stagger for typewriter], motion.ts §22 mirror; entranceMs = max(60, preset.entranceMs·(subtle?1.2:1))).
  - Entrance recipes (from-state static + \t target, per the emission spec): fade-rise/slide-y/push → alpha-only (y-offset not representable inline — documented deviation); word-pop → {\fscx40\alpha&HFF&} + α over entranceMs/2.5 + two-phase 115%→100% overshoot (entranceMs → 1.3×entranceMs); scale-slam → 240%→100% + α in min(120, entranceMs); blur-focus → \blur(10·energy) 112% 50%α → \blur0 100% α; flash → 190% (emphasis) / 140% (else) → 100%; clip-wipe → per-line animated \clip(x1,y1,x1,y2)→(x1,y1,x2,y2) (the proven v1.17 mask-wipe recipe) + per-word α ramp entranceMs/1.6; push → fade-rise entrance + push-out dim \t(phraseLastEnd+60, +600, α·0.55) on every word (motion.ts 1−0.45·since mirror; upward drift dropped — \pos not animatable).
  - Emphasis event (§21): emphasisMotion scale-punch/pulse → extra \t(ws, ws+200, \fscx118\fscy118) + \t(ws+200, ws+320, \fscx100) at the word's spoken moment (ws = window-relative − Dialogue start; SKIPPED when the punch fully passed before the window opened). Hold active-word/active-accent → skipped (too stateful — documented deviation; emphasis words carry the accent color statically instead).
  - Exit: \fad(0, exitMs) on every Dialogue — ONLY when the composition truly ends inside the window (comp.endMs ≤ winEnd); a window-clipped composition continues in the next chunk/segment, so a fade at every window tail would PULSE at each boundary (deliberate deviation, stronger than the spec's unconditional \fad — chunk continuity). Words whose entrance completed before winStart emit their SETTLED state (no \t/\move — mid-composition continuity across chunk/segment cuts); exit variants (slide-down/scale-out/collapse/push-out) → fade approximation.

Work Log:
- INVESTIGATION (Explore agent): rustEligible pushed reason "captions" whenever captionSettings.enabled && subtitleCues.length > 0 — EVERY captioned export (the default transcription flow, auto-enabled word mode) routed to CLI. Also found: kinetic typography (v1.19 flagship) → "kinetic-captions" gate; every new headline (stackStyle default) → "stack-text" gate.
- NEW rust-engine/src/captions.rs (~1400 lines incl. tests): the native caption renderer.
  - Contract: timeline.captions = { fontKey, fontSizePx (output-scaled), colors, bg, shadow, textTransform, letterSpacing, alignment, position, positionY, maxWidthFrac, wordMode, animation, cues[{startMs,endMs,text,words}] } — Electron precomputes every concrete number (the renderer already resolved the CaptionPreset into ipcCaptionSettings; no preset knowledge in Rust).
  - Layout mirrors drawCaption (native.ts, the preview ground truth): 1.25 line height, balanced triangle wrap (2- and 3-line splits, decreasing-width preference, greedy fallback), position anchor + positionY inset, 4% safe margins, per-line alignment, bg box padded around the widest line (1×1 solid fill bitmap scaled to the rect — rounded radius dropped like libass).
  - Animation: FULL port of computeWordTransform (captionAnimations.ts) — all 24 animations (pop-in, slide-up, bounce-in, scale-pulse, fade-through, typewriter, reveal, wave, jitter, shake, drift, slam, glitch, spin-in, flip-in, elastic, color-cycle, spotlight, swing, squash, zoom-words, tracking-in, blur-in, heartbeat) with the exact constants + easings (easeOutBack/Cubic/Elastic/Quart, seededRand hash) — expressed as per-word (dest-rect scale, offset, alpha) transforms + color variants (normal / highlight / 4-color cycle palette) + spotlight box rects. Documented deviations: rotation → dropped (rect blits can't rotate), clipLeft → alpha ramp, letterSpacing anim → dropped, glitch RGB-split → x-jump+flicker.
  - Word modes: "off" (full block, whole-cue transform), "word" (karaoke — ACTIVE word highlighted, mirroring activeWordIndex's most-recent-started semantics = the canvas, NOT the ASS \k fill-everything), "word-only" (single active word centered), "stack" (spoken words stack, older dimmed &HA0&-style 0.37, max 8 rows, block grows).
  - Bitmap cache: (word text, color variant) → pre-rasterized strip (baseline-aligned constant strip height; shadow offset + 8-dir outline baked under the fill) — speech vocabulary dedups, per-frame work is pure blit math.
- FIXED A REAL GLYPH RENDERING BUG (found by the smoke test): the max-blend `if a > rgba[d+3]` let the outline pass's identical coverage SUPPRESS the fill pass — glyphs rendered in the OUTLINE color (shadow/outline-only). Fix: the fill pass is the topmost layer and writes unconditionally. The SAME bug existed in text.rs (headline renderer — headlines with outlines rendered as outline-colored text since v1.16!). Fixed both.
- export.rs: captions::prepare once (font + layout + bitmap cache, Arc'd through the producer); build_frame_job extends text_layers with layers_at(pc, now_ms) AFTER texts, BEFORE watermark (the ASS emission order: headline < cue); caption failure degrades to captions-less export (never fails the export). FF_DEBUG_CAPTIONS=1 dumps per-frame layer rects.
- compositor/cpu.rs: text strips may now be SCALED (animated words, 1×1 fills) — added scale_rgba (fast_image_resize bilinear + nearest + solid-fill fast path) used when bitmap dims ≠ dest dims. GPU path already scaled via dest-rect transforms.
- timeline.rs: CaptionsTimeline + CaptionCue + CaptionWord structs (camelCase serde, all-defaulted).
- rust-engine-router.js: the `captions` gate is GONE (only kinetic typography captions stay gated to the CLI/libass compositor); buildRustCaptions() maps ipcCaptionSettings + subtitleCues → the captions block with hScale pre-scaling; CAPTION_FONT_FILES map (ASS fontName → OS font file, [regular, bold] per family — Montserrat/Inter/Roboto→Arial, Bebas→Impact, Playfair→Georgia, etc.); fonts.caption resolution; rustGateReason(opts) exported.
- main.js: after runRustExport returns null, computes cliEngineReason via RUST.rustGateReason and threads `engineReason` into EVERY export-progress event.
- Header.tsx + types.ts: the engine badge shows the routing reason — "FFmpeg CLI · kinetic-captions" with amber reason text + tooltips explaining how to get the native engine back.
- native.ts: ipcCaptionSettings now carries maxWidth (the wrap fraction — Rust needs it explicit; ASS derived it from margins).
- VERIFIED: cargo check + clippy ZERO warnings in captions.rs (Rust toolchain installed in the sandbox this session); 6 cargo unit tests PASS (layout/baseline/modes/animations/bg fills); **built the Linux .node and ran REAL end-to-end exports** — /home/z/captest/caption-smoke.js: 15/15 PASS with pixel-level frame verification (karaoke gold active-word highlight, staggered pop-in entrances, word-only single word, stack growth + dimming, purple bg box, after-cue cleanup); regression: v2-feature-test.js PASS (transitions/audio/frames intact), yuv-color-test.js PASS (compositor exactness intact), gate harness 24/24 PASS (captions+animations+word modes eligible; kinetic/stack-text/slide/loudnorm still gated; font resolution + pre-scaled styles).

Task ID: 2-a
Agent: tts-backend
Task: Edge-TTS backend — word-level timings, long-form (≤200k words) chunked synthesis with progress/cancel, voice styles; new tts:synthesize-long / tts:cancel-long / tts:read-audio IPC + preload APIs.

Work Log:
- Read worklog tail (v1.24.0 Flow Night state), then read ALL of electron/edge-tts.js (1194 lines), the main.js TTS IPC block (~L1293–1507), and preload.js fully before editing.
- edge-tts.js WORD TIMINGS: live-protocol debugging revealed the service delivers Path:audio.metadata as TEXT frames (header\r\n\r\nJSON body, same layout as turn.end) — NOT binary frames as the old comment assumed. Interception added in BOTH handleServerText (live path) and handleServerBinary (belt-and-braces). New collectWordMetadata() parses {"Metadata":[{"Type":"WordBoundary","Data":{Offset,Duration,text:{Text}}}]} with 100-ns→ms conversion (/10,000), accepts the flattened Data.Text shape, skips malformed entries non-fatally; new decodeBinaryMessage() extracted from handleServerBinary (shared with tests). Per-attempt `words` array (retry-safe: fresh array per attempt); attemptSynthesis now resolves {mp3, words}; synthesize() returns {filePath, bytes, bytesLen, words}.
- edge-tts.js STYLES: normalizeStyleOption() validates a short token /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/ (hyphen allowed for real style names like narration-professional); buildSsml wraps the prosody in <mstts:express-as style='…'> inside <voice> and declares xmlns:mstts on <speak> ONLY when a style is used (styleless SSML stays byte-identical for dub-workflow). Style flows through synthesize + synthesizeLong + normalizeSynthOptions.
- edge-tts.js VOICE LIST: normalizeVoice passes through optional raw LocalName and StyleList (comma-separated string → string[] styleList) via conditional spread — absent fields don't appear, existing field names untouched (verified live: 322-entry catalog renders identical shape).
- edge-tts.js synthesizeLong: splitTextIntoChunks() (≤2800 chars; cut preference sentence terminators [.!?…।؟\n] incl. Devanagari danda + Urdu ؟ → last whitespace → hard cut; only chunk edges trimmed) + synthesizeLong() — fires ALL chunk synthesize() calls up front (the existing 3-slot FIFO queue serializes), results land by INDEX (parallel completion never reorders), one caller-facing {abort} fans out to per-chunk abortRefs (dub-workflow's childAbortRef pattern; works while queued AND in flight; one chunk failing cancels siblings and propagates the real error, pure cancels reject with the standard cancelledError), onProgress({phase:"synth",chunkIndex,chunkCount,charsDone,totalChars}) after each completion, merge = ordered Buffer.concat + global word offsets (chunkStartMs += bytesLen/6000·1000 — 48 kbps CBR MP3), words sorted by offset, returns {bytes, bytesLen, words, chunkCount}. Input cap MAX_LONG_TEXT_LEN = 1.5M chars.
- NECESSARY FIX: the old flat 25 s attempt watchdog killed healthy 2800-char chunks (measured: 2800 chars ⇒ ~17 s solo, ~3× under 3-way concurrency ⇒ >25 s). Scaled it: 25 s + 20 ms/char of text (healthy streams measured ~6 ms/char; short dub lines still die at the 25 s baseline). Without this, synthesizeLong could never complete.
- main.js (edits anchored strictly inside the TTS block): tts:synthesize-long (mutex via ttsLongState like dubState → "A long text-to-speech job is already running"; 1.5M-char cap → "Text too long — 200k word limit reached"; clamps rate −95..+100 / pitch ±100 / volume −100..+100; progress on "tts:progress" {runId, phase synth→probe→done, chunkIndex/chunkCount/charsDone/totalChars, status, durationMs}; writes ttslong_<ts>.mp3 into tempDir; ffprobe duration with bytes/6000 fallback; reply {filePath, fileName, bytesLen, durationMs, words, chunkCount} — NO raw bytes), tts:cancel-long (runId-equality-guarded abort → {ok, running}), tts:read-audio (path.resolve + startsWith(tempDir+sep) security check, 200 MB cap, → {bytes}); tts:synthesize widened (rate ±95, pitch ±100, volume ±100, style passthrough, words in reply, 3000-char cap kept); tts:preview cap 120→300 + style passthrough.
- preload.js: ttsSynthesizeLong(p) / ttsCancelLong(runId) / ttsReadAudio(filePath) / onTtsProgress(cb) with unsubscribe (exact onWhisperProgress pattern); ttsPreview/ttsSynthesize doc comments updated (≤300, style, words).
- VERIFIED live (network to speech.platform.bing.com reachable — TLS 40 ms): bun require test → "function function function"; /tmp/tts-smoke.js (bun) → synthesize returns 8 words [{text,offsetMs,durationMs}] monotonic (service strips trailing punctuation in word texts — "world," arrives as "world"); synthesizeLong 12,000 chars → 5 chunks, 5,735,520 merged bytes, 2,206 GLOBAL words monotonic and within total duration (last word end 955,047 ms vs 955,920 ms total), 5 progress events (charsDone cumulative 11996/12000), merged MP3 frame-sync valid; abort of a 60k-char run mid-flight rejects "Edge TTS synthesis cancelled" (.cancelled=true) incl. queued chunks; live Hindi hi-IN-SwaraNeural long run → danda-aware chunking, 32 Devanagari word timings. Parser unit test (buildWsFrame→createWsFrameParser→decodeBinaryMessage→collectWordMetadata, split-frame feeding) passes on the final file.
- PROTOCOL FINDINGS (documented in code comments): (1) word metadata arrives as TEXT frames; (2) the FREE Read Aloud endpoint REJECTS <mstts:express-as> with close code 1007 "SSML is invalid" (xmlns:mstts on <speak> alone is ACCEPTED — isolated by test), and its 322-voice live catalog ships ZERO StyleList fields — so styles are correctly dormant on this service and only activate when a voice advertises them, per the spec's gating; the SSML construction itself is spec-exact for when an endpoint supports it.
- tsc: my 3 files contribute 0 errors (electron/*.js are outside the type-checked scope); the 9 pre-existing baseline errors are unchanged (test-export-parity×3, engine×2, gpu-worker-client×3, chroma×1). During my session a PARALLEL agent's in-flight src/app/page.tsx edit produced transient errors (30 → 8: audioTrack/audioRef/MediaPanel/SettingsPanel prop mismatches — music/voiceover UI wiring, NOT TTS-backend) and the matching transient GET / 500 in dev.log; same precedent as the v1.23 sweep-C mid-edit window. node --check clean on all 3 files. Nothing committed.

Stage Summary:
- electron/edge-tts.js now returns per-word timings from every synthesis, synthesizes up to 1.5 M characters (~200 k words) as ONE merged MP3 with global word timings + progress + full cancellation, and supports voice styles (mstts:express-as, validated, namespace-gated) + styleList/localName voice-catalog passthrough. One deliberate deviation: the attempt watchdog scales with text length (25 s + 20 ms/char) — the flat 25 s made ≥2800-char chunks impossible; style tokens allow hyphen for real style names.
- main.js gained tts:synthesize-long (mutex + progress + ffprobe + guarded reply), tts:cancel-long (runId-guarded), tts:read-audio (tempDir-containment security check, 200 MB cap); tts:synthesize/tts:preview updated per spec. preload.js exposes the four new renderer APIs.
- Flags for the lead/frontend agent (2-b): word texts arrive PUNCTUATION-STRIPPED from the service ("world," → "world"); style UI should only offer styles from a voice's styleList (always empty on the current free service — the endpoint rejects express-as outright); ~12 k chars took ~27 s wall (≈440 chars/s through the 3-slot queue), so 200 k-word runs are an hours-scale job — progress + cancel are load-bearing.

Task ID: 12
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User complaint — Dubbing tab missing latest features in the WEB preview; create a SEPARATE tab for simple Text-to-Speech; fix TTS language/voice selection + add voice presets

Work Log:
- Investigated current state: v1.26 Dub Studio stages (transcribe→script→dub) exist in code (commit 1ce7dd2) with IPC handlers dub:transcribe/dub:script/dub:start + TtsStudioSection, BUT everything is gated on window.electronAPI → in the web preview the user sees ONLY an inert TTS card (empty voice/language pickers — useTtsVoices returns null without IPC) and NO DubSection at all (inElectron && gate hides it). That is exactly the complaint.
- Verified sandbox capabilities: electron/edge-tts.js runs standalone in Node (322 voices, hi-IN pair, word-level timings from synthesize); ffmpeg-static has a Linux binary; z-ai ASR works on WAV (text only — NO timestamps → word timing must come from ffmpeg silence segmentation + char-weighted distribution); z-ai LLM available for script generation.
- DESIGN DECISION: dual-transport speech layer — when window.electronAPI exists use IPC (packaged app), else fetch new Next.js API routes that run Edge-TTS + ffmpeg + z-ai SDK server-side. API contracts:
  - GET  /api/tts/voices        → { voices, pairs, languages }
  - POST /api/tts/synthesize    → { bytes(base64 mp3), bytesLen, mimeType, words, chunkCount }
  - POST /api/dub/transcribe    (multipart files + meta JSON) → DubTranscriptResult { language, totalMs, wordCount, utterances[{startMs,endMs,text,words[]}] }
  - POST /api/dub/script        → DubScriptResult { targetLanguage, targetLanguageName, speakerCount, lines[{i,startMs,endMs,speaker,sourceText,translatedText}], warnings } (default targetLanguage "hi")
  - POST /api/dub/synthesize    → DubTrackResult-shaped { speakers[], segments[{...,bytes base64,ttsDurMs,speedApplied}], wavPaths: [], warnings }
  - GET  /api/dub/models        → { models: [], langNames, provider: "web" }
- UI plan: NEW dock/settings tab "tts" (Text to Speech) hosting TtsStudioSection + VoiceoverSection with FIXED pickers (language dropdown grouped from catalog, voice dropdown filtered by language, voice PRESETS built-in + user-saved) and working web synthesis; Dubbing tab keeps DubSection (un-gated, web-transport) + ScriptWriter(electron) + TextRemoval. page.tsx dub handlers route through the transport; web dubSources built from timeline File blobs.

Stage Summary:
- Root cause confirmed; contracts fixed; delegation: Task 12-a backend API routes (subagent), 12-b frontend transport + tab restructure (main), 12-c integration test (main), 12-d version bump 1.26.0 + build + release (main)

Devanagari/Nastaliq script hints, fence-stripping/first{..last} robust parse, ONE stricter retry, then batch-translate fallback, then source-text+warning; timings/i/speaker always rebuilt from input, speakerCount clamped 1..6, speaker 0..5); dub-synthesize.ts (voice resolution chain singleVoice→voices[speaker]→pairs[locale] female@0/male@rest→any catalog locale voice→first voice; natural duration from last WordBoundary else CBR bytesLen/6ms; duration-fit re-synthesis ONCE at ratePct=min(60,…) when ttsDur>slot×1.12 with speedApplied=1+rate/100 and "line i exceeds its slot" warning; short audio kept; per-line failures→skip+warning, all-fail→500).
- ROUTES (all runtime="nodejs", NextRequest/NextResponse, {ok:false,error} with proper 400/413/500, no stack leaks): GET /api/tts/voices (voices+pairs+languages grouped by locale language part, DUB_LANG_NAMES→Intl.DisplayNames fallback, sorted); POST /api/tts/synthesize (text ≤400k chars, voice ShortName-shape check→400, unknown-but-shaped voice sanitized to same-locale catalog voice, rate/pitch clamped −50..+100/±50, volume 0..2 linear → engine volumePct=(v−1)·100, ≤2800→synthesize else synthesizeLongText, response {bytes base64, bytesLen, mimeType audio/mpeg, words, chunkCount}); POST /api/dub/transcribe (multipart files+meta, mkdtemp ff-dub-* cleanup in finally, per-file meta startMs/endMs|null, no-meta→back-to-back cumulative timeline); POST /api/dub/script (utterances ≤500, targetLanguage default "hi", sourceLanguage/style hints); POST /api/dub/synthesize (lines ≤500, singleVoice null-overrides, empty targetLocale derived from language via pairs); GET /api/dub/models ({models:[], langNames, provider:"web", web:true}).
- Z-AI SDK AVAILABILITY: not in package.json — only in the bun global install. Task ID: 14 (v1.27.1 hotfix — launch crash)
Agent: main (Z.ai Code)
Task: User: installed v1.27.0 crashes at launch — "SyntaxError: Identifier 'hasMusic' has already been declared" at electron/export-graph.js:2042 (Uncaught Exception in the main process)

Work Log:
- ROOT CAUSE: buildConcatArgs (electron/export-graph.js) had TWO `const hasMusic` declarations in the SAME function scope — line 2008 (`!!o.audioPath`, the v5.2 original) and line 2042 (`!!o.audioPath || hasMusicClips`, the v1.25 multi-music upgrade meant to REPLACE it). V8 rejects redeclarations at parse time → main.js line 28's require() of export-graph.js killed the whole main process at launch. Introduced in commit db1f54f (v1.25.0).
- WHY EVERY GATE MISSED IT: eslint.config.mjs ignores `electron/**`; tsc has ignoreBuildErrors + the electron folder is plain JS; `next build` never parses electron/ (copied into the ASAR verbatim); the web dev preview never loads electron/*.js. The installed app's first launch was the FIRST time V8 parsed the file.
- FIX: removed the stale line-2008 declaration (nothing used hasMusic between 2008 and 2041; every downstream usage assumes the broader v1.25 definition — loopMusic/else-if/musicCount are hasMusicClips-guarded, the amix gate at 2074 needs the OR, buildAudioMixGraph's hasMusic is `!hasMusicClips && hasMusic`).
- REGRESSION: 7-scenario argv test on buildConcatArgs (legacy single music + loop stream_loop, multi-music 2 inputs, clip-audio amix graph, masterMix early return, multi-music+amix, legacy music+amix, silent project no -c:a) — ALL PASS.
- NEW GATE: scripts/check-electron-js.js — `node --check` (full V8 parse, the exact error class main hits at require time) over all 13 electron/*.js files; wired into package.json `electron:build` (before electron-builder) AND `lint` (`eslint . && node scripts/check-electron-js.js`). All 13 files clean.
- REBUILD v1.27.1 (version bump: package.json + Header BUILD_VERSION + layout title): static export (api-move wrapper) → gpu-worker → wasm → parse gate → ffmpeg (cached) → engine RUST_ENGINE_SKIP=1 (reused CI binary) → NSIS. dist/FrameFuse Setup 1.27.1.exe 220,189,276 B + blockmap + latest.yml, rcedit 1.27.1.0.
- PACKAGED-FILE VERIFICATION: extracted electron/export-graph.js from the new ASAR → node --check PASSES; engine binary inside ASAR sha256 abbea0b1593ccd630218e669bcf50f6f63ced63227edf003853d8e0db4a19aef (EXACT match to the CI smoke-tested artifact).
- PUBLISHED: commit 459631c pushed → release 403424740 tag v1.27.1 "FrameFuse v1.27.1 — launch-crash hotfix", 3 assets uploaded, THREE-WAY sha512 VERIFIED (P7cBWlyUDy1S…, size exact). LIVE: https://github.com/Ziruax/framefuse/releases/tag/v1.27.1
- v1.27.0 release body PATCHED with a ⚠️ top banner: "This build crashes at launch … use v1.27.1 instead" (API PATCH 200, verified in release list).
- Dev server restarted (double-fork daemon, HTTP 200, title "FrameFuse v1.27.1 — Windows Desktop Video Studio", 0 page errors).

Stage Summary:
- v1.27.0 was DOA (main-process parse crash); v1.27.1 SHIPS the fix + a permanent parse gate on every electron JS file (build + lint), so a redeclaration/syntax error in the main-process folder can never reach a packaged build again.

- Standing operational notes remain: re-copy z-ai-web-dev-sdk after node_modules regeneration; electron static exports must route through scripts/build-next-electron.js while src/app/api exists.

Content/timing are shared-plan by design (deterministic §36); the drift lives in the STYLE + ANIMATION + ROUTING layers.

- DIVERGENCES FOUND & FIXED (export side, preview is truth):
  1. Base color: preview kinetic painter = customColor || #FFFFFF; ASS used cs.textColor (= customColor || CAPTION-PRESET textColor — cream/amber presets burned the wrong color) and Rust read cs.customColor which the payload NEVER carried (always white, ignoring the user's custom color). FIX: native.ts payload now ships customColor (1 additive line) + main.js kinetic emit passes (cs && cs.customColor) || "#FFFFFF" (the Rust router's existing cs.customColor read now resolves).
  2. Supporting-tier alpha: motion.ts hardcodes 0.82; the ASS emitter used preset.supportAlpha (0.78/0.66 — a dead field in the painter). FIX: kinetic-ass.js settledAlpha mirrors motion.ts's 0.82.
  3. Style stroke + tracking: the painter draws NO outline and measures/draws with NO letter-spacing (trackingFrac is dead in the painter); the ASS Style carried Outline=2px + Spacing=trackingFrac*fontPx → burned words visibly heavier/wider than the preview AND libass re-flowed lines away from the measured geometry. FIX: Outline=0, Spacing=0, shadow kept (3px·hScale @ 0.55 — same depth the Rust strips bake).
  4. fade-rise/slide-y/push entrances: previously degraded to alpha-only inline lines ("y-motion not representable inline" — true inline, but per-word \move IS). FIX: they now ride the per-word \move path with the exact motion.ts e=0 offsets (36·energy·roleEnergy rise / ±42·energy alternate / 30·energy push); per-word branch also gained the push-dim tags.
  5. push-out dimming guard: motion.ts arms it for push entrance OR push-out exit; the emitter only checked entrance. FIX: guard mirrors motion.ts.
  6. Per-word \b threshold now uses the word's EFFECTIVE measured weight (geo weight — inline emphasis bumps), not the phrase weight.
  7. FAST MODE (constrained-CPU 720p downscale): smart-pipeline ASS pieces are built at the DOWNSCALED PlayRes while the kinetic geometry stayed measured at the requested dims → every \pos/\fs in the wrong coordinate space (misplaced + oversized captions). FIX: kineticGeoList rescaled by the same s factor as the watermark rects (deep map over block/lines/words fields).
